      {
        "tool_use_id": "toolu_01RnWcFq3HeaS54qsuS4G5op",
        "type": "tool_result",
        "content": "     1\t```markdown\n     2\t# v0.0.4 - NIP-90 Swarm Inference & DVM Capabilities\n     3\t\n     4\tThis is an **alpha pre-release** version for developers.\n     5\t\n     6\tThis release marks a significant step towards a decentralized AI ecosystem by fully integrating NIP-90 Data Vending Machine (DVM) capabilities. Users can now not only consume AI services from the network (paid in Bitcoin via Lightning) but also offer their own compute resources as DVMs. The new \"Swarm Inference\" feature allows seamless toggling between local models and these network-based DVMs directly within the chat interface.\n     7\t\n     8\t### Features\n     9\t\n    10\t-   **NEW: Full NIP-90 Integration (Client & Provider):**\n    11\t    -   **Consume DVM Services:** Request AI inference (e.g., text generation) from NIP-90 DVMs on the Nostr network.\n    12\t    -   **Become a DVM Provider (\"Sell Compute\"):** Offer your local Ollama models as a service to the network, earning Bitcoin.\n    13\t        -   Connect your Spark wallet and Ollama instance.\n    14\t        -   Configure DVM settings (identity, relays, supported job kinds, pricing, model parameters).\n    15\t        -   Automatically process NIP-90 job requests (Kind 5050/5100), generate Lightning invoices, and send results.\n    16\t        -   Includes DVM Job History & Statistics pane.\n    17\t-   **NEW: Swarm Inference:**\n    18\t    -   Seamlessly switch between local AI models (via Ollama) and remote NIP-90 DVMs directly in the `AgentChatPane`.\n    19\t    -   UI for selecting preferred AI provider and model.\n    20\t-   **NEW: Bitcoin Lightning Payments for AI Services:**\n    21\t    -   Integrated Spark Wallet for paying NIP-90 DVM job invoices.\n    22\t    -   Automatic invoice generation for DVM providers.\n    23\t    -   Automatic payment verification for DVM providers.\n    24\t-   **NEW: NIP-90 User Interface:**\n    25\t    -   `Nip90RequestForm`: Manually create and publish encrypted NIP-90 job requests.\n    26\t    -   `Nip90GlobalFeedPane`: View recent NIP-90 events (requests, results, feedback) from connected relays.\n    27\t    -   `Nip90ConsumerChatPane`: A dedicated chat interface to interact with specific NIP-90 DVMs.\n    28\t-   **Enhanced AI Backend:**\n    29\t    -   Refactored AI services to be provider-agnostic (`AgentLanguageModel.Tag`).\n    30\t    -   `ChatOrchestratorService` manages AI provider selection and enables future resilience features (like `AiPlan`).\n    31\t-   **Existing Features Maintained:**\n    32\t    -   NIP-28 public chat channels.\n    33\t    -   Hand tracking for UI interaction (can be toggled).\n    34\t\n    35\t### Installing\n    36\t\n    37\tNo binaries yet. Run it locally following our [local development instructions.](https://github.com/OpenAgentsInc/commander#running-a-dev-build)\n    38\t\n    39\t**Key Requirements for this release:**\n    40\t-   **Ollama:** Must be running locally for local model inference and if you intend to \"Sell Compute\". The default model is `gemma3:1b` (`ollama pull gemma3:1b`).\n    41\t-   **Spark Wallet:** Required for paying NIP-90 DVMs or for acting as a DVM provider. Ensure your Spark wallet is configured and funded.\n    42\t-   **Nostr Identity:** A Nostr keypair is needed for NIP-90 interactions (both as consumer and provider) and NIP-28 chats.\n    43\t\n    44\tIf the main branch is too far ahead, you can use this release's commit via `git checkout e0f1a2b3c4d5e6f7a8b9c0d1e2f3a4b5c6d7e8f9` (Example commit hash).\n    45\t\n    46\t![004](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/OpenAgentsInc/commander/main/docs/assets/004_swarm_inference.png)\n    47\t*(Caption: The new Agent Chat pane showing a dropdown to select between local Ollama models and remote NIP-90 DVMs, with a DVM Job History pane also visible showcasing processed network jobs.)*\n    48\t\n    49\t## What's Changed\n    50\t\n    51\tThis release represents a major overhaul of the AI backend and introduces significant new functionality related to NIP-90 and decentralized AI.\n    52\t\n    53\t-   **NIP-90 Implementation (Phases 4, 6, 7):**\n    54\t    -   Full client-side capabilities to request and pay for jobs from NIP-90 DVMs.\n    55\t    -   Robust DVM provider (\"Sell Compute\") implementation allowing users to offer services using their local Ollama and Spark Wallet.\n    56\t    -   Services include `NIP90Service`, `Kind5050DVMService`.\n    57\t-   **Swarm Inference & Provider Abstraction:**\n    58\t    -   The `AgentLanguageModel.Tag` abstraction now supports multiple providers.\n    59\t    -   `ChatOrchestratorService` dynamically selects the AI provider based on user choice or configuration.\n    60\t    -   Ollama integration refactored as a provider under the new AI backend.\n    61\t-   **Spark Wallet Integration:**\n    62\t    -   `SparkService` enhanced for DVM payment processing (invoice creation and payment).\n    63\t    -   Secure handling of wallet interactions through Effect-TS services.\n    64\t-   **UI Enhancements:**\n    65\t    -   `AgentChatPane` updated with provider/model selection.\n    66\t    -   New panes for NIP-90 interactions: `Nip90RequestForm`, `Nip90ConsumerChatPane`, `Nip90GlobalFeedPane`.\n    67\t    -   New panes for \"Sell Compute\": `SellComputePane`, `DVMSettingsDialog`, `DvmJobHistoryPane`.\n    68\t-   **Backend Refinements:**\n    69\t    -   Addressed stale runtime reference issues ensuring services use up-to-date configurations (Fix `023`).\n    70\t    -   Improved IPC handling for Ollama and other main-process services.\n    71\t    -   Enhanced telemetry across AI and DVM services.\n    72\t-   **Documentation:**\n    73\t    -   New documentation for NIP-90 (`docs/NIP90.md`) and Selling Compute (`docs/SELLING_COMPUTE.md`).\n    74\t    -   Updated AI Roadmap phases to reflect new architecture.\n    75\t\n    76\t**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/OpenAgentsInc/commander/compare/v0.0.3...v0.0.4\n    77\t```\n    78\t"
      }
    ]
  },
  "session_id": "91e1e982-1c20-4391-80c0-3570ab5a44aa"
}

        "content": "     1\t# Coder Pane System Architecture\n     2\t\n     3\t## Table of Contents\n     4\t1. [Overview](#overview)\n     5\t2. [User Interactions](#user-interactions)\n    17\t    5.1. [Creating New Chat Sessions](#creating-new-chat-sessions)\n    18\t    5.2. [Loading Chat History](#loading-chat-history)\n    19\t    5.3. [Sending Messages](#sending-messages)\n    20\t    5.4. [Keyboard Shortcuts](#keyboard-shortcuts)\n    21\t6. [Database Integration](#database-integration)\n    22\t7. [Styling and Appearance](#styling-and-appearance)\n    23\t8. [Testing Considerations](#testing-considerations)\n    24\t9. [Future Enhancements](#future-enhancements)\n    25\t\n    26\t## 1. Overview\n    27\t\n    28\tThe Coder Pane is a specialized chat interface within OpenAgents Commander that provides an AI-powered coding assistant experience through Claude Code. It's designed as a draggable, resizable pane that can be opened multiple times with independent sessions, allowing users to maintain multiple coding conversations simultaneously.\n    29\t\n    30\t**Key Features:**\n    31\t\n    32\t- **AI-Powered Assistance:** Direct integration with Claude Code API for intelligent coding help\n    33\t- **Tool Use Display:** Visual representation of Claude's tool calls (file operations, searches, etc.)\n    34\t- **Session Persistence:** All conversations are saved to a local database and can be reloaded\n    35\t- **Multi-Instance Support:** Multiple coder panes can run independently with different sessions\n    36\t- **ProseMirror Editor:** Rich text input with multi-line support (Shift+Enter for new lines) and dynamic height resizing\n    37\t- **History Management:** Dropdown menu showing recent chat sessions with quick-load functionality\n    38\t- **Streaming Responses:** Real-time display of Claude's responses as they're generated\n    39\t- **Keyboard Navigation:** Escape to close, Cmd/Ctrl+Click for opening in new panes\n    40\t\n    41\t## 2. Architecture Diagram\n    42\t\n    43\t```\n    44\t+--------------------------------+      +--------------------------------+      +-----------------------------+\n    45\t| User Actions                   |----->| CoderPane Component            |----->| useCoderChat Hook           |\n    46\t| (Type message, click button,   |      | (UI orchestration,             |      | (Chat logic, state mgmt,    |\n    47\t|  select from history, Esc key) |      |  menu handling, telemetry)     |      |  API calls, DB operations)  |\n    48\t+--------------------------------+      +--------------------------------+      +-------------+---------------+\n    49\t                                                    |                                         |\n    50\t                                                    | Renders                                 | Updates\n    51\t                                                    v                                         v\n    52\t                                          +---------+----------+                    +---------+----------+\n    53\t                                          | UI Components      |                    | Local State        |\n    54\t                                          | - CoderMessageList |                    | - messages[]       |\n    55\t                                          | - CoderMessage     |                    | - isLoading        |\n    56\t                                          | - CoderProseMirror |                    | - focusKey         |\n    57\t                                          +--------------------+                    | - sessionIdRef     |\n    58\t                                                    |                               +--------------------+\n    59\t                                                    | Displays                                |\n    60\t                                                    v                                         v\n    61\t                                          +--------------------+                    +--------------------+\n    62\t                                          | Content Types      |                    | External Services  |\n    63\t                                          | - Text messages    |<------------------>| - Claude Code API  |\n    64\t                                          | - Tool calls       |                    | - DatabaseService  |\n    65\t                                          | - Tool results     |                    | - TelemetryService |\n    66\t                                          | - Streaming status |                    +--------------------+\n    67\t                                          +--------------------+\n    68\t```\n    69\t\n    70\t## 3. Component & Module Breakdown\n    71\t\n    72\t### 3.1. Core Hook (`useCoderChat`)\n    73\t\n    74\tLocated at `src/hooks/coder/useCoderChat.ts`, this custom hook encapsulates all chat-related logic:\n    75\t\n    76\t```typescript\n    77\texport interface ChatMessage {\n    78\t  id: string;\n    79\t  role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system';\n    80\t  content: string; // Full textual content for DB\n    81\t  parts?: Array< // For UI rendering\n    82\t    | { type: 'text'; text: string }\n    83\t    | { type: 'tool_call'; id: string; name: string; input: Record<string, any> }\n    84\t    | { type: 'tool_result'; tool_use_id: string; content: any; isError?: boolean; isLoading?: boolean }\n    85\t  >;\n    86\t  timestamp: number;\n    87\t  isStreaming?: boolean;\n    88\t}\n    89\t\n    90\tinterface UseCoderChatProps {\n    91\t  paneId: string;\n    92\t  initialSessionId?: string;\n    93\t}\n    94\t\n    95\texport function useCoderChat(props: UseCoderChatProps) {\n    96\t  // Returns:\n    97\t  // - messages: ChatMessage[]\n    98\t  // - isLoading: boolean\n    99\t  // - focusKey: number (for triggering editor re-focus)\n   100\t  // - sendMessage: (content: string) => Promise<void>\n   101\t  // - loadMessagesForSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<void>\n   102\t  // - clearMessagesAndSession: () => string (returns new session ID)\n   103\t}\n   104\t```\n   105\t\n   106\t**Key Responsibilities:**\n   107\t\n   108\t- **State Management:** Maintains local React state for messages, loading status, and focus control\n   109\t- **Session Tracking:** Uses refs to track current session ID and prevent duplicate loads\n   110\t- **Message Operations:** Provides functions to add, update, and clear messages\n   111\t- **API Integration:** Handles streaming chat with Claude Code via `window.electronAPI.claudeCode.streamChat`\n   112\t- **Database Operations:** Loads historical messages and saves new conversations using Effect and DatabaseService\n   113\t- **Stream Management:** Tracks active streams with cancellation support\n   114\t\n   115\t**Implementation Details:**\n   116\t\n   117\t- Uses Effect library for functional programming patterns in database operations\n   118\t- Implements intelligent message rehydration from database, handling both legacy and structured content formats\n   119\t- Manages tool call/result pairing for proper UI display\n   120\t- Provides automatic session ID generation with timestamp and random suffix pattern\n   121\t\n   122\t### 3.2. Main Component (`CoderPane`)\n   123\t\n   124\tLocated at `src/components/coder/CoderPane.tsx`, this is the main orchestrator component:\n   125\t\n   126\t```typescript\n   127\texport interface CoderPaneProps {\n   128\t  paneId: string;\n   129\t  sessionId?: string; // Passed from pane content\n   130\t  titleBarButtonsRef?: { current: any; set: (value: any) => void }; // For dynamic title bar\n   131\t}\n   132\t```\n   133\t\n   134\t**Key Responsibilities:**\n   135\t\n   136\t- **Hook Integration:** Uses `useCoderChat` for all chat functionality\n   137\t- **UI Layout:** Structures the pane with message list and input areas\n   138\t- **Menu Management:** Creates and manages the History dropdown menu in the title bar\n   139\t- **Telemetry:** Tracks user interactions (opening, closing, new chats)\n   140\t- **Keyboard Handling:** Implements Escape key to close functionality\n   141\t- **Multi-Pane Support:** Handles Cmd/Ctrl+Click for opening items in new panes\n   142\t\n   143\t**Title Bar Features:**\n   144\t\n   145\t- **New Chat Button:** Creates fresh session, supports Cmd/Ctrl+Click for new pane\n   146\t- **History Menu:** Shows 5 most recent sessions with timestamps and preview\n   147\t  - Format: \"MM/DD/YY HH:MM | session-id...\"\n   148\t  - Normal click: Load in current pane\n   149\t  - Cmd/Ctrl+Click: Open in new pane\n   150\t\n   151\t### 3.3. Input Component (`CoderProseMirrorInput`)\n   152\t\n   153\tLocated at `src/components/coder/CoderProseMirrorInput.tsx`, handles rich text input:\n   154\t\n   155\t```typescript\n   156\tinterface CoderProseMirrorInputProps {\n   157\t  onSubmit: (text: string) => void;\n   158\t  disabled?: boolean;\n   159\t  focusKey?: number; // To trigger re-focus\n   160\t}\n   161\t```\n   162\t\n   163\t**Key Features:**\n   164\t\n   165\t- **ProseMirror Integration:** Lazy-loaded for performance\n   166\t- **Multi-line Support:** Enter submits, Shift+Enter adds line break\n   167\t- **Dynamic Height:** Starts at 1 line (44px), expands up to 5 lines (124px), then scrolls\n   168\t- **Auto-focus:** Maintains focus after operations like new chat or history load\n   169\t- **Text Serialization:** Preserves line breaks and formatting when extracting text\n   170\t- **Visual Feedback:** Opacity changes when disabled during streaming\n   171\t\n   172\t**Implementation Details:**\n   173\t\n   174\t- Uses `@handlewithcare/react-prosemirror` for React integration\n   175\t- Custom keymap for Enter/Shift+Enter handling\n   176\t- Document serialization preserves paragraph structure and hard breaks\n   177\t- RequestAnimationFrame used for reliable focus management\n   178\t- Dynamic sizing: `minHeight: 44px`, `maxHeight: 124px`, `overflowY: auto`\n   179\t- Height calculations based on line height (20px) + padding (24px total)\n   180\t\n   181\t### 3.4. Message Display Components\n   182\t\n   183\t#### CoderMessage (`src/components/coder/CoderMessage.tsx`)\n   184\t\n   185\tRenders individual chat messages with appropriate formatting:\n   186\t\n   187\t```typescript\n   188\tinterface CoderMessageProps {\n   189\t  message: ChatMessage;\n   190\t  index: number;\n   191\t}\n   192\t```\n   193\t\n   194\t**Features:**\n   195\t\n   196\t- **Part-based Rendering:** Handles text, tool calls, and tool results differently\n   197\t- **Copy Functionality:** Hover-activated copy button for full message content\n   198\t- **Streaming Indicator:** Shows \"Claude Code is working\" with spinner\n   199\t- **Role-based Styling:** Different appearance for user vs assistant messages\n   200\t\n   201\t#### CoderMessageList (`src/components/coder/CoderMessageList.tsx`)\n   202\t\n   203\tManages the scrollable message container:\n   204\t\n   205\t```typescript\n   206\tinterface CoderMessageListProps {\n   207\t  messages: ChatMessage[];\n   208\t  containerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;\n   209\t  handleScroll: () => void;\n   210\t  handleTouchStart: () => void;\n   211\t  isStreamingLastMessage: boolean;\n   212\t}\n   213\t```\n   214\t\n   215\t**Features:**\n   216\t\n   217\t- **Auto-scroll Integration:** Works with `useAutoScroll` hook\n   218\t- **System Message Filtering:** Hides internal system messages from view\n   219\t- **Responsive Layout:** Centers content with max-width constraint\n   220\t\n   221\t### 3.5. Tool Display Components\n   222\t\n   223\t#### ToolCallDisplay (`src/components/coder/ToolCallDisplay.tsx`)\n   224\t\n   225\tShows Claude's tool invocations:\n   226\t\n   227\t- Displays tool name and parameters in a collapsible format\n   228\t- Loading state with spinner while tool executes\n   229\t- Syntax highlighting for JSON parameters\n   230\t\n   231\t#### ToolResultDisplay (`src/components/coder/ToolResultDisplay.tsx`)\n   232\t\n   233\tShows results from tool executions:\n   234\t\n   235\t- Success/error state visualization\n   236\t- Collapsible content for large results\n   237\t- Special formatting for different content types (file contents, errors, etc.)\n   238\t\n   239\t## 4. State Management & Data Flow\n   240\t\n   241\t### 4.1. Session Management\n   242\t\n   243\tSessions follow a specific lifecycle:\n   244\t\n   245\t1. **Creation:** Generated with format `ui-coder-${timestamp}-${random}`\n   246\t2. **Storage:** Saved in component refs and pane content\n   247\t3. **Persistence:** Written to database after each message exchange\n   248\t4. **Restoration:** Loaded from database with full message history and tool executions\n   249\t\n   250\t**Session ID Flow:**\n   251\t- `initialSessionId` (prop) → `sessionIdRef` (current) → `lastLoadedSessionIdRef` (tracking)\n   252\t- Prevents duplicate loads and ensures consistent state\n   253\t\n   254\t### 4.2. Message Streaming\n   255\t\n   256\tThe streaming process for Claude's responses:\n   257\t\n   258\t1. **Initiation:** User message triggers `sendMessage()`\n   259\t2. **Placeholder Creation:** Assistant message with `isStreaming: true`\n   260\t3. **Chunk Processing:** \n   261\t   ```typescript\n   262\t   // Chunks can be plain text or structured data\n   263\t   { type: 'text', text: '...' }\n   264\t   { type: 'tool_call', id: '...', name: '...', parameters: {...} }\n   265\t   { type: 'tool_result', tool_use_id: '...', content: {...} }\n   266\t   ```\n   267\t4. **Message Updates:** Parts array built incrementally, content accumulated for DB\n   268\t5. **Completion:** `isStreaming: false`, final state saved\n   269\t\n   270\t### 4.3. Tool Call Handling\n   271\t\n   272\tTool calls follow a specific pattern:\n   273\t\n   274\t1. **Tool Call Receipt:** Structured data with unique ID\n   275\t2. **UI Update:** `ToolCallDisplay` shows immediately with loading state\n   276\t3. **Result Association:** Results matched by `tool_use_id`\n   277\t4. **Display Update:** `ToolResultDisplay` replaces loading state\n   278\t\n   279\t**Database Storage:**\n   280\t- Tool calls stored in `tool_calls_json` column\n   281\t- Results stored in separate `tool_executions` table\n   282\t- Rehydration reconstructs the full interaction\n   283\t\n   284\t## 5. User Interactions\n   285\t\n   286\t### 5.1. Creating New Chat Sessions\n   287\t\n   288\t**Standard New Chat:**\n   289\t1. Click \"New Chat\" button\n   290\t2. Current session saved to `closedPanePositions`\n   291\t3. New session ID generated\n   292\t4. Messages cleared, editor focused\n   293\t\n   294\t**New Chat in New Pane (Cmd/Ctrl+Click):**\n   295\t1. Hold Cmd/Ctrl while clicking\n   296\t2. New pane created at offset position\n   297\t3. Fresh session started in new pane\n   298\t4. Original pane unchanged\n   299\t\n   300\t### 5.2. Loading Chat History\n   301\t\n   302\t**From History Menu:**\n   303\t1. Click \"History\" dropdown\n   304\t2. Menu fetches 5 most recent sessions\n   305\t3. Select session to load\n   306\t4. Messages and tool executions restored\n   307\t5. Scroll to bottom, focus editor\n   308\t\n   309\t**Via Session ID (Programmatic):**\n   310\t1. Pane created with specific `sessionId` in content\n   311\t2. `useEffect` triggers load on mount\n   312\t3. Full conversation restored\n   313\t\n   314\t### 5.3. Sending Messages\n   315\t\n   316\t1. Type in ProseMirror editor (multi-line supported)\n   317\t2. Press Enter to send (Shift+Enter for new line)\n   318\t3. Message added to UI immediately\n   319\t4. Streaming response begins\n   320\t5. Tool calls/results displayed as received\n   321\t6. Final message saved to database\n   322\t\n   323\t### 5.4. Keyboard Shortcuts\n   324\t\n   325\t- **Escape:** Close the coder pane (when focused)\n   326\t- **Enter:** Send message\n   327\t- **Shift+Enter:** New line in message\n   328\t- **Cmd/Ctrl+Click:** Open items in new pane (buttons and menu items)\n   329\t\n   330\t## 6. Database Integration\n   331\t\n   332\tThe Coder Pane integrates deeply with the DatabaseService:\n   333\t\n   334\t**Tables Used:**\n   335\t- `sessions`: Stores session metadata\n   336\t- `messages`: Stores all messages with role, content, and timestamps\n   337\t- `tool_executions`: Stores tool call details and results\n   338\t\n   339\t**Key Operations:**\n   340\t\n   341\t```typescript\n   342\t// Loading messages for a session\n   343\tdb.getMessagesForSession(sessionId, limit)\n   344\t\n   345\t// Getting tool executions for messages\n   346\tdb.getToolCallsForMessage(messageId)\n   347\t\n   348\t// Saving is handled by Claude Code bridge service\n   349\t// which writes to DB after processing\n   350\t```\n   351\t\n   352\t**Message Content Formats:**\n   353\t\n   354\t1. **Plain Text:** Simple string content\n   355\t2. **Structured Parts:** JSON with `{ parts: [...] }` for complex messages\n   356\t3. **Legacy Tool Calls:** Separate `tool_calls_json` column\n   357\t4. **Modern Format:** Array of typed parts in content field\n   358\t\n   359\t## 7. Styling and Appearance\n   360\t\n   361\tThe Coder Pane has distinct visual styling:\n   362\t\n   363\t**Container:**\n   364\t- Black background with high opacity\n   365\t- Full height flex layout\n   366\t- Custom scrollbar styling\n   367\t\n   368\t**Messages:**\n   369\t- User messages: 80% max width, white border\n   370\t- Assistant messages: 100% width, no border\n   371\t- Monospace font throughout\n   372\t- White text on black background\n   373\t\n   374\t**Tool Displays:**\n   375\t- Muted borders and backgrounds\n   376\t- Collapsible with smooth transitions\n   377\t- Syntax highlighting for code/JSON\n   378\t\n   379\t**Editor:**\n   380\t- Dynamic height: starts at 44px (1 line), expands up to 124px (5 lines)\n   381\t- Internal scrolling when content exceeds 5 lines\n   382\t- White border\n   383\t- 750px max width (matching message area)\n   384\t- Opacity feedback when disabled\n   385\t\n   386\t**CSS-in-JS Styles:**\n   387\tThe component includes embedded styles for prose formatting, ensuring consistent appearance across all message types.\n   388\t\n   389\t## 8. Testing Considerations\n   390\t\n   391\t**Unit Tests:**\n   392\t- `useCoderChat` hook logic (state updates, API calls)\n   393\t- Message parsing and formatting functions\n   394\t- Session ID generation and validation\n   395\t\n   396\t**Integration Tests:**\n   397\t- Database operations (save/load cycles)\n   398\t- Streaming message handling\n   399\t- Tool call/result association\n   400\t\n   401\t**E2E Tests:**\n   402\t- Full conversation flow\n   403\t- History menu interactions\n   404\t- Multi-pane scenarios\n   405\t- Keyboard shortcuts\n   406\t\n   407\t**Key Test Scenarios:**\n   408\t1. Create new chat → Send message → Receive response → Close → Reopen → Verify persistence\n   409\t2. Open history → Load session → Verify content → Send follow-up\n   410\t3. Cmd+Click new chat → Verify independent panes\n   411\t4. Stream interruption → Verify graceful handling\n   412\t\n   413\t## 9. Future Enhancements\n   414\t\n   415\t**Potential Improvements:**\n   416\t\n   417\t1. **Search Functionality:** Search across all chat history\n   418\t2. **Export Options:** Save conversations as markdown/PDF\n   419\t3. **Model Selection:** Choose between different Claude models\n   420\t4. **Code Block Actions:** Copy, run, or save code snippets directly\n   421\t5. **Collaborative Sessions:** Share sessions with team members\n   422\t6. **Voice Input:** Dictate messages instead of typing\n   423\t7. **Theme Customization:** Light mode support, font size options\n   424\t8. **Performance Optimization:** Virtual scrolling for very long conversations\n   425\t9. **Advanced Tool Displays:** Interactive file trees, inline editors\n   426\t10. **Session Templates:** Pre-configured prompts for common tasks\n   427\t\n   428\t**Technical Debt:**\n   429\t- Consolidate message format handling (legacy vs modern)\n   430\t- Optimize database queries for large histories\n   431\t- Implement message pagination for performance\n   432\t- Add retry logic for failed API calls\n   433\t\n   434\tThe Coder Pane represents a sophisticated integration of AI assistance, real-time streaming, persistent storage, and intuitive UI design, providing developers with a powerful tool for AI-assisted coding within the OpenAgents Commander environment."
      }
    ]

Overview\n    27\t\n    28\tThe Coder Pane is a specialized chat interface within OpenAgents Commander that provides an AI-powered coding assistant experience through Claude Code. It's designed as a draggable, resizable pane that can be opened multiple times with independent sessions, allowing users to maintain multiple coding conversations simultaneously.\n    29\t\n    30\t**Key Features:**\n    31\t\n    32\t- **AI-Powered Assistance:** Direct integration with Claude Code API for intelligent coding help\n    33\t- **Tool Use Display:** Visual representation of Claude's tool calls (file operations, searches, etc.)\n    34\t- **Session Persistence:** All conversations are saved to a local database and can be reloaded\n    35\t- **Multi-Instance Support:** Multiple coder panes can run independently with different sessions\n    36\t- **ProseMirror Editor:** Rich text input with multi-line support (Shift+Enter for new lines) and dynamic height resizing\n    37\t- **History Management:** Dropdown menu showing recent chat sessions with quick-load functionality\n    38\t- **Streaming Responses:** Real-time display of Claude's responses as they're generated\n    39\t- **Keyboard Navigation:** Escape to close, Cmd/Ctrl+Click for opening in new panes\n    40\t\n    41\t## 2. Architecture Diagram\n    42\t\n    43\t```\n    44\t+--------------------------------+      +--------------------------------+      +-----------------------------+\n    45\t| User Actions                   |----->| CoderPane Component            |----->| useCoderChat Hook           |\n    46\t| (Type message, click button,   |      | (UI orchestration,             |      | (Chat logic, state mgmt,    |\n    47\t|  select from history, Esc key) |      |  menu handling, telemetry)     |      |  API calls, DB operations)  |\n    48\t+--------------------------------+      +--------------------------------+      +-------------+---------------+\n    49\t                                                    |                                         |\n    50\t                                                    | Renders                                 | Updates\n    51\t                                                    v                                         v\n    52\t                                          +---------+----------+                    +---------+----------+\n    53\t                                          | UI Components      |                    | Local State        |\n    54\t                                          | - CoderMessageList |                    | - messages[]       |\n    55\t                                          | - CoderMessage     |                    | - isLoading        |\n    56\t                                          | - CoderProseMirror |                    | - focusKey         |\n    57\t                                          +--------------------+                    | - sessionIdRef     |\n    58\t                                                    |                               +--------------------+\n    59\t                                                    | Displays                                |\n    60\t                                                    v                                         v\n    61\t                                          +--------------------+                    +--------------------+\n    62\t                                          | Content Types      |                    | External Services  |\n    63\t                                          | - Text messages    |<------------------>| - Claude Code API  |\n    64\t                                          | - Tool calls       |                    | - DatabaseService  |\n    65\t                                          | - Tool results     |                    | - TelemetryService |\n    66\t                                          | - Streaming status |                    +--------------------+\n    67\t                                          +--------------------+\n    68\t```\n    69\t\n    70\t## 3. Component & Module Breakdown\n    71\t\n    72\t### 3.1. Core Hook (`useCoderChat`)\n    73\t\n    74\tLocated at `src/hooks/coder/useCoderChat.ts`, this custom hook encapsulates all chat-related logic:\n    75\t\n    76\t```typescript\n    77\texport interface ChatMessage {\n    78\t  id: string;\n    79\t  role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system';\n    80\t  content: string; // Full textual content for DB\n    81\t  parts?: Array< // For UI rendering\n    82\t    | { type: 'text'; text: string }\n    83\t    | { type: 'tool_call'; id: string; name: string; input: Record<string, any> }\n    84\t    | { type: 'tool_result'; tool_use_id: string; content: any; isError?: boolean; isLoading?: boolean }\n    85\t  >;\n    86\t  timestamp: number;\n    87\t  isStreaming?: boolean;\n    88\t}\n    89\t\n    90\tinterface UseCoderChatProps {\n    91\t  paneId: string;\n    92\t  initialSessionId?: string;\n    93\t}\n    94\t\n    95\texport function useCoderChat(props: UseCoderChatProps) {\n    96\t  // Returns:\n    97\t  // - messages: ChatMessage[]\n    98\t  // - isLoading: boolean\n    99\t  // - focusKey: number (for triggering editor re-focus)\n   100\t  // - sendMessage: (content: string) => Promise<void>\n   101\t  // - loadMessagesForSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<void>\n   102\t  // - clearMessagesAndSession: () => string (returns new session ID)\n   103\t}\n   104\t```\n   105\t\n   106\t**Key Responsibilities:**\n   107\t\n   108\t- **State Management:** Maintains local React state for messages, loading status, and focus control\n   109\t- **Session Tracking:** Uses refs to track current session ID and prevent duplicate loads\n   110\t- **Message Operations:** Provides functions to add, update, and clear messages\n   111\t- **API Integration:** Handles streaming chat with Claude Code via `window.electronAPI.claudeCode.streamChat`\n   112\t- **Database Operations:** Loads historical messages and saves new conversations using Effect and DatabaseService\n   113\t- **Stream Management:** Tracks active streams with cancellation support\n   114\t\n   115\t**Implementation Details:**\n   116\t\n   117\t- Uses Effect library for functional programming patterns in database operations\n   118\t- Implements intelligent message rehydration from database, handling both legacy and structured content formats\n   119\t- Manages tool call/result pairing for proper UI display\n   120\t- Provides automatic session ID generation with timestamp and random suffix pattern\n   121\t\n   122\t### 3.2. Main Component (`CoderPane`)\n   123\t\n   124\tLocated at `src/components/coder/CoderPane.tsx`, this is the main orchestrator component:\n   125\t\n   126\t```typescript\n   127\texport interface CoderPaneProps {\n   128\t  paneId: string;\n   129\t  sessionId?: string; // Passed from pane content\n   130\t  titleBarButtonsRef?: { current: any; set: (value: any) => void }; // For dynamic title bar\n   131\t}\n   132\t```\n   133\t\n   134\t**Key Responsibilities:**\n   135\t\n   136\t- **Hook Integration:** Uses `useCoderChat` for all chat functionality\n   137\t- **UI Layout:** Structures the pane with message list and input areas\n   138\t- **Menu Management:** Creates and manages the History dropdown menu in the title bar\n   139\t- **Telemetry:** Tracks user interactions (opening, closing, new chats)\n   140\t- **Keyboard Handling:** Implements Escape key to close functionality\n   141\t- **Multi-Pane Support:** Handles Cmd/Ctrl+Click for opening items in new panes\n   142\t\n   143\t**Title Bar Features:**\n   144\t\n   145\t- **New Chat Button:** Creates fresh session, supports Cmd/Ctrl+Click for new pane\n   146\t- **History Menu:** Shows 5 most recent sessions with timestamps and preview\n   147\t  - Format: \"MM/DD/YY HH:MM | session-id...\"\n   148\t  - Normal click: Load in current pane\n   149\t  - Cmd/Ctrl+Click: Open in new pane\n   150\t\n   151\t### 3.3. Input Component (`CoderProseMirrorInput`)\n   152\t\n   153\tLocated at `src/components/coder/CoderProseMirrorInput.tsx`, handles rich text input:\n   154\t\n   155\t```typescript\n   156\tinterface CoderProseMirrorInputProps {\n   157\t  onSubmit: (text: string) => void;\n   158\t  disabled?: boolean;\n   159\t  focusKey?: number; // To trigger re-focus\n   160\t}\n   161\t```\n   162\t\n   163\t**Key Features:**\n   164\t\n   165\t- **ProseMirror Integration:** Lazy-loaded for performance\n   166\t- **Multi-line Support:** Enter submits, Shift+Enter adds line break\n   167\t- **Dynamic Height:** Starts at 1 line (44px), expands up to 5 lines (124px), then scrolls\n   168\t- **Auto-focus:** Maintains focus after operations like new chat or history load\n   169\t- **Text Serialization:** Preserves line breaks and formatting when extracting text\n   170\t- **Visual Feedback:** Opacity changes when disabled during streaming\n   171\t\n   172\t**Implementation Details:**\n   173\t\n   174\t- Uses `@handlewithcare/react-prosemirror` for React integration\n   175\t- Custom keymap for Enter/Shift+Enter handling\n   176\t- Document serialization preserves paragraph structure and hard breaks\n   177\t- RequestAnimationFrame used for reliable focus management\n   178\t- Dynamic sizing: `minHeight: 44px`, `maxHeight: 124px`, `overflowY: auto`\n   179\t- Height calculations based on line height (20px) + padding (24px total)\n   180\t\n   181\t### 3.4. Message Display Components\n   182\t\n   183\t#### CoderMessage (`src/components/coder/CoderMessage.tsx`)\n   184\t\n   185\tRenders individual chat messages with appropriate formatting:\n   186\t\n   187\t```typescript\n   188\tinterface CoderMessageProps {\n   189\t  message: ChatMessage;\n   190\t  index: number;\n   191\t}\n   192\t```\n   193\t\n   194\t**Features:**\n   195\t\n   196\t- **Part-based Rendering:** Handles text, tool calls, and tool results differently\n   197\t- **Copy Functionality:** Hover-activated copy button for full message content\n   198\t- **Streaming Indicator:** Shows \"Claude Code is working\" with spinner\n   199\t- **Role-based Styling:** Different appearance for user vs assistant messages\n   200\t\n   201\t#### CoderMessageList (`src/components/coder/CoderMessageList.tsx`)\n   202\t\n   203\tManages the scrollable message container:\n   204\t\n   205\t```typescript\n   206\tinterface CoderMessageListProps {\n   207\t  messages: ChatMessage[];\n   208\t  containerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;\n   209\t  handleScroll: () => void;\n   210\t  handleTouchStart: () => void;\n   211\t  isStreamingLastMessage: boolean;\n   212\t}\n   213\t```\n   214\t\n   215\t**Features:**\n   216\t\n   217\t- **Auto-scroll Integration:** Works with `useAutoScroll` hook\n   218\t- **System Message Filtering:** Hides internal system messages from view\n   219\t- **Responsive Layout:** Centers content with max-width constraint\n   220\t\n   221\t### 3.5. Database Integration\n   331\t\n   332\tThe Coder Pane integrates deeply with the DatabaseService:\n   333\t\n   334\t**Tables Used:**\n   335\t- `sessions`: Stores session metadata\n   336\t- `messages`: Stores all messages with role, content, and timestamps\n   337\t- `tool_executions`: Stores tool call details and results\n   338\t\n   339\t**Key Operations:**\n   340\t\n   341\t```typescript\n   342\t// Loading messages for a session\n   343\tdb.getMessagesForSession(sessionId, limit)\n   344\t\n   345\t// Getting tool executions for messages\n   346\tdb.getToolCallsForMessage(messageId)\n   347\t\n   348\t// Saving is handled by Claude Code bridge service\n   349\t// which writes to DB after processing\n   350\t```\n   351\t\n   352\t**Message Content Formats:**\n   353\t\n   354\t1. **Plain Text:** Simple string content\n   355\t2. **Structured Parts:** JSON with `{ parts: [...] }` for complex messages\n   356\t3. **Legacy Tool Calls:** Separate `tool_calls_json` column\n   357\t4. **Modern Format:** Array of typed parts in content field\n   358\t\n   359\t## 7. Styling and Appearance\n   360\t\n   361\tThe Coder Pane has distinct visual styling:\n   362\t\n   363\t**Container:**\n   364\t- Black background with high opacity\n   365\t- Full height flex layout\n   366\t- Custom scrollbar styling\n   367\t\n   368\t**Messages:**\n   369\t- User messages: 80% max width, white border\n   370\t- Assistant messages: 100% width, no border\n   371\t- Monospace font throughout\n   372\t- White text on black background\n   373\t\n   374\t**Tool Displays:**\n   375\t- Muted borders and backgrounds\n   376\t- Collapsible with smooth transitions\n   377\t- Syntax highlighting for code/JSON\n   378\t\n   379\t**Editor:**\n   380\t- Dynamic height: starts at 44px (1 line), expands up to 124px (5 lines)\n   381\t- Internal scrolling when content exceeds 5 lines\n   382\t- White border\n   383\t- 750px max width (matching message area)\n   384\t- Opacity feedback when disabled\n   385\t\n   386\t**CSS-in-JS Styles:**\n   387\tThe component includes embedded styles for prose formatting, ensuring consistent appearance across all message types.\n   388\t\n   389\t## 8. Testing Considerations\n   390\t\n   391\t**Unit Tests:**\n   392\t- `useCoderChat` hook logic (state updates, API calls)\n   393\t- Message parsing and formatting functions\n   394\t- Session ID generation and validation\n   395\t\n   396\t**Integration Tests:**\n   397\t- Database operations (save/load cycles)\n   398\t- Streaming message handling\n   399\t- Tool call/result association\n   400\t\n   401\t**E2E Tests:**\n   402\t- Full conversation flow\n   403\t- History menu interactions\n   404\t- Multi-pane scenarios\n   405\t- Keyboard shortcuts\n   406\t\n   407\t**Key Test Scenarios:**\n   408\t1. Future Enhancements\n   414\t\n   415\t**Potential Improvements:**\n   416\t\n   417\t1. **Search Functionality:** Search across all chat history\n   418\t2. **Export Options:** Save conversations as markdown/PDF\n   419\t3. **Model Selection:** Choose between different Claude models\n   420\t4. **Code Block Actions:** Copy, run, or save code snippets directly\n   421\t5. **Collaborative Sessions:** Share sessions with team members\n   422\t6. **Voice Input:** Dictate messages instead of typing\n   423\t7. **Theme Customization:** Light mode support, font size options\n   424\t8. **Performance Optimization:** Virtual scrolling for very long conversations\n   425\t9. **Advanced Tool Displays:** Interactive file trees, inline editors\n   426\t10. **Session Templates:** Pre-configured prompts for common tasks\n   427\t\n   428\t**Technical Debt:**\n   429\t- Consolidate message format handling (legacy vs modern)\n   430\t- Optimize database queries for large histories\n   431\t- Implement message pagination for performance\n   432\t- Add retry logic for failed API calls\n   433\t\n   434\tThe Coder Pane represents a sophisticated integration of AI assistance, real-time streaming, persistent storage, and intuitive UI design, providing developers with a powerful tool for AI-assisted coding within the OpenAgents Commander environment."}]

Overview\\n    27\\t\\n    28\\tThe Coder Pane is a specialized chat interface within OpenAgents Commander that provides an AI-powered coding assistant experience through Claude Code. It's designed as a draggable, resizable pane that can be opened multiple times with independent sessions, allowing users to maintain multiple coding conversations simultaneously.\\n    29\\t\\n    30\\t**Key Features:**\\n    31\\t\\n    32\\t- **AI-Powered Assistance:** Direct integration with Claude Code API for intelligent coding help\\n    33\\t- **Tool Use Display:** Visual representation of Claude's tool calls (file operations, searches, etc.)\\n    34\\t- **Session Persistence:** All conversations are saved to a local database and can be reloaded\\n    35\\t- **Multi-Instance Support:** Multiple coder panes can run independently with different sessions\\n    36\\t- **ProseMirror Editor:** Rich text input with multi-line support (Shift+Enter for new lines) and dynamic height resizing\\n    37\\t- **History Management:** Dropdown menu showing recent chat sessions with quick-load functionality\\n    38\\t- **Streaming Responses:** Real-time display of Claude's responses as they're generated\\n    39\\t- **Keyboard Navigation:** Escape to close, Cmd/Ctrl+Click for opening in new panes\\n    40\\t\\n    41\\t## 2. Architecture Diagram\\n    42\\t\\n    43\\t```\\n    44\\t+--------------------------------+      +--------------------------------+      +-----------------------------+\\n    45\\t| User Actions                   |----->| CoderPane Component            |----->| useCoderChat Hook           |\\n    46\\t| (Type message, click button,   |      | (UI orchestration,             |      | (Chat logic, state mgmt,    |\\n    47\\t|  select from history, Esc key) |      |  menu handling, telemetry)     |      |  API calls, DB operations)  |\\n    48\\t+--------------------------------+      +--------------------------------+      +-------------+---------------+\\n    49\\t                                                    |                                         |\\n    50\\t                                                    | Renders                                 | Updates\\n    51\\t                                                    v                                         v\\n    52\\t                                          +---------+----------+                    +---------+----------+\\n    53\\t                                          | UI Components      |                    | Local State        |\\n    54\\t                                          | - Core Hook (`useCoderChat`)\\n    73\\t\\n    74\\tLocated at `src/hooks/coder/useCoderChat.ts`, this custom hook encapsulates all chat-related logic:\\n    75\\t\\n    76\\t```typescript\\n    77\\texport interface ChatMessage {\\n    78\\t  id: string;\\n    79\\t  role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system';\\n    80\\t  content: string; // Full textual content for DB\\n    81\\t  parts?: Array< // For UI rendering\\n    82\\t    | { type: 'text'; text: string }\\n    83\\t    | { type: 'tool_call'; id: string; name: string; input: Record<string, any> }\\n    84\\t    | { type: 'tool_result'; tool_use_id: string; content: any; isError?: boolean; isLoading?: boolean }\\n    85\\t  >;\\n    86\\t  timestamp: number;\\n    87\\t  isStreaming?: boolean;\\n    88\\t}\\n    89\\t\\n    90\\tinterface UseCoderChatProps {\\n    91\\t  paneId: string;\\n    92\\t  initialSessionId?: string;\\n    93\\t}\\n    94\\t\\n    95\\texport function useCoderChat(props: UseCoderChatProps) {\\n    96\\t  // Returns:\\n    97\\t  // - messages: ChatMessage[]\\n    98\\t  // - isLoading: boolean\\n    99\\t  // - focusKey: number (for triggering editor re-focus)\\n   100\\t  // - sendMessage: (content: string) => Promise<void>\\n   101\\t  // - loadMessagesForSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<void>\\n   102\\t  // - clearMessagesAndSession: () => string (returns new session ID)\\n   103\\t}\\n   104\\t```\\n   105\\t\\n   106\\t**Key Responsibilities:**\\n   107\\t\\n   108\\t- **State Management:** Maintains local React state for messages, loading status, and focus control\\n   109\\t- **Session Tracking:** Uses refs to track current session ID and prevent duplicate loads\\n   110\\t- **Message Operations:** Provides functions to add, update, and clear messages\\n   111\\t- **API Integration:** Handles streaming chat with Claude Code via `window.electronAPI.claudeCode.streamChat`\\n   112\\t- **Database Operations:** Loads historical messages and saves new conversations using Effect and DatabaseService\\n   113\\t- **Stream Management:** Tracks active streams with cancellation support\\n   114\\t\\n   115\\t**Implementation Details:**\\n   116\\t\\n   117\\t- Uses Effect library for functional programming patterns in database operations\\n   118\\t- Implements intelligent message rehydration from database, handling both legacy and structured content formats\\n   119\\t- Manages tool call/result pairing for proper UI display\\n   120\\t- Provides automatic session ID generation with timestamp and random suffix pattern\\n   121\\t\\n   122\\t### 3.2. Main Component (`CoderPane`)\\n   123\\t\\n   124\\tLocated at `src/components/coder/CoderPane.tsx`, this is the main orchestrator component:\\n   125\\t\\n   126\\t```typescript\\n   127\\texport interface CoderPaneProps {\\n   128\\t  paneId: string;\\n   129\\t  sessionId?: string; // Passed from pane content\\n   130\\t  titleBarButtonsRef?: { current: any; set: (value: any) => void }; // For dynamic title bar\\n   131\\t}\\n   132\\t```\\n   133\\t\\n   134\\t**Key Responsibilities:**\\n   135\\t\\n   136\\t- **Hook Integration:** Uses `useCoderChat` for all chat functionality\\n   137\\t- **UI Layout:** Structures the pane with message list and input areas\\n   138\\t- **Menu Management:** Creates and manages the History dropdown menu in the title bar\\n   139\\t- **Telemetry:** Tracks user interactions (opening, closing, new chats)\\n   140\\t- **Keyboard Handling:** Implements Escape key to close functionality\\n   141\\t- **Multi-Pane Support:** Handles Cmd/Ctrl+Click for opening items in new panes\\n   142\\t\\n   143\\t**Title Bar Features:**\\n   144\\t\\n   145\\t- **New Chat Button:** Creates fresh session, supports Cmd/Ctrl+Click for new pane\\n   146\\t- **History Menu:** Shows 5 most recent sessions with timestamps and preview\\n   147\\t  - Format: \\\"MM/DD/YY HH:MM | session-id...\\\"\\n   148\\t  - Normal click: Load in current pane\\n   149\\t  - Cmd/Ctrl+Click: Open in new pane\\n   150\\t\\n   151\\t### 3.3. Input Component (`CoderProseMirrorInput`)\\n   152\\t\\n   153\\tLocated at `src/components/coder/CoderProseMirrorInput.tsx`, handles rich text input:\\n   154\\t\\n   155\\t```typescript\\n   156\\tinterface CoderProseMirrorInputProps {\\n   157\\t  onSubmit: (text: string) => void;\\n   158\\t  disabled?: boolean;\\n   159\\t  focusKey?: number; // To trigger re-focus\\n   160\\t}\\n   161\\t```\\n   162\\t\\n   163\\t**Key Features:**\\n   164\\t\\n   165\\t- **ProseMirror Integration:** Lazy-loaded for performance\\n   166\\t- **Multi-line Support:** Enter submits, Shift+Enter adds line break\\n   167\\t- **Dynamic Height:** Starts at 1 line (44px), expands up to 5 lines (124px), then scrolls\\n   168\\t- **Auto-focus:** Maintains focus after operations like new chat or history load\\n   169\\t- **Text Serialization:** Preserves line breaks and formatting when extracting text\\n   170\\t- **Visual Feedback:** Opacity changes when disabled during streaming\\n   171\\t\\n   172\\t**Implementation Details:**\\n   173\\t\\n   174\\t- Uses `@handlewithcare/react-prosemirror` for React integration\\n   175\\t- Custom keymap for Enter/Shift+Enter handling\\n   176\\t- Document serialization preserves paragraph structure and hard breaks\\n   177\\t- RequestAnimationFrame used for reliable focus management\\n   178\\t- Dynamic sizing: `minHeight: 44px`, `maxHeight: 124px`, `overflowY: auto`\\n   179\\t- Height calculations based on line height (20px) + padding (24px total)\\n   180\\t\\n   181\\t### 3.4. Overview\\n    27\\t\\n    28\\tThe Coder Pane is a specialized chat interface within OpenAgents Commander that provides an AI-powered coding assistant experience through Claude Code. It's designed as a draggable, resizable pane that can be opened multiple times with independent sessions, allowing users to maintain multiple coding conversations simultaneously.\\n    29\\t\\n    30\\t**Key Features:**\\n    31\\t\\n    32\\t- **AI-Powered Assistance:** Direct integration with Claude Code API for intelligent coding help\\n    33\\t- **Tool Use Display:** Visual representation of Claude's tool calls (file operations, searches, etc.)\\n    34\\t- **Session Persistence:** All conversations are saved to a local database and can be reloaded\\n    35\\t- **Multi-Instance Support:** Multiple coder panes can run independently with different sessions\\n    36\\t- **ProseMirror Editor:** Rich text input with multi-line support (Shift+Enter for new lines) and dynamic height resizing\\n    37\\t- **History Management:** Dropdown menu showing recent chat sessions with quick-load functionality\\n    38\\t- **Streaming Responses:** Real-time display of Claude's responses as they're generated\\n    39\\t- **Keyboard Navigation:** Escape to close, Cmd/Ctrl+Click for opening in new panes\\n    40\\t\\n    41\\t## 2. Architecture Diagram\\n    42\\t\\n    43\\t```\\n    44\\t+--------------------------------+      +--------------------------------+      +-----------------------------+\\n    45\\t| User Actions                   |----->| CoderPane Component            |----->| useCoderChat Hook           |\\n    46\\t| (Type message, click button,   |      | (UI orchestration,             |      | (Chat logic, state mgmt,    |\\n    47\\t|  select from history, Esc key) |      |  menu handling, telemetry)     |      |  API calls, DB operations)  |\\n    48\\t+--------------------------------+      +--------------------------------+      +-------------+---------------+\\n    49\\t                                                    |                                         |\\n    50\\t                                                    | Renders                                 | Updates\\n    51\\t                                                    v                                         v\\n    52\\t                                          +---------+----------+                    +---------+----------+\\n    53\\t                                          | UI Components      |                    | Local State        |\\n    54\\t                                          | - CoderMessageList |                    | - messages[]       |\\n    55\\t                                          | - CoderMessage     |                    | - isLoading        |\\n    56\\t                                          | - CoderProseMirror |                    | - focusKey         |\\n    57\\t                                          +--------------------+                    | - sessionIdRef     |\\n    58\\t                                                    |                               +--------------------+\\n    59\\t                                                    | Displays                                |\\n    60\\t                                                    v                                         v\\n    61\\t                                          +--------------------+                    +--------------------+\\n    62\\t                                          | Content Types      |                    | External Services  |\\n    63\\t                                          | - Text messages    |<------------------>| - Claude Code API  |\\n    64\\t                                          | - Tool calls       |                    | - DatabaseService  |\\n    65\\t                                          | - Tool results     |                    | - TelemetryService |\\n    66\\t                                          | - Streaming status |                    +--------------------+\\n    67\\t                                          +--------------------+\\n    68\\t```\\n    69\\t\\n    70\\t## 3. Component & Module Breakdown\\n    71\\t\\n    72\\t### 3.1. Core Hook (`useCoderChat`)\\n    73\\t\\n    74\\tLocated at `src/hooks/coder/useCoderChat.ts`, this custom hook encapsulates all chat-related logic:\\n    75\\t\\n    76\\t```typescript\\n    77\\texport interface ChatMessage {\\n    78\\t  id: string;\\n    79\\t  role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system';\\n    80\\t  content: string; // Full textual content for DB\\n    81\\t  parts?: Array< // For UI rendering\\n    82\\t    | { type: 'text'; text: string }\\n    83\\t    | { type: 'tool_call'; id: string; name: string; input: Record<string, any> }\\n    84\\t    | { type: 'tool_result'; tool_use_id: string; content: any; isError?: boolean; isLoading?: boolean }\\n    85\\t  >;\\n    86\\t  timestamp: number;\\n    87\\t  isStreaming?: boolean;\\n    88\\t}\\n    89\\t\\n    90\\tinterface UseCoderChatProps {\\n    91\\t  paneId: string;\\n    92\\t  initialSessionId?: string;\\n    93\\t}\\n    94\\t\\n    95\\texport function useCoderChat(props: UseCoderChatProps) {\\n    96\\t  // Returns:\\n    97\\t  // - messages: ChatMessage[]\\n    98\\t  // - isLoading: boolean\\n    99\\t  // - focusKey: number (for triggering editor re-focus)\\n   100\\t  // - sendMessage: (content: string) => Promise<void>\\n   101\\t  // - loadMessagesForSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<void>\\n   102\\t  // - clearMessagesAndSession: () => string (returns new session ID)\\n   103\\t}\\n   104\\t```\\n   105\\t\\n   106\\t**Key Responsibilities:**\\n   107\\t\\n   108\\t- **State Management:** Maintains local React state for messages, loading status, and focus control\\n   109\\t- **Session Tracking:** Uses refs to track current session ID and prevent duplicate loads\\n   110\\t- **Message Operations:** Provides functions to add, update, and clear messages\\n   111\\t- **API Integration:** Handles streaming chat with Claude Code via `window.electronAPI.claudeCode.streamChat`\\n   112\\t- **Database Operations:** Loads historical messages and saves new conversations using Effect and DatabaseService\\n   113\\t- **Stream Management:** Tracks active streams with cancellation support\\n   114\\t\\n   115\\t**Implementation Details:**\\n   116\\t\\n   117\\t- Uses Effect library for functional programming patterns in database operations\\n   118\\t- Implements intelligent message rehydration from database, handling both legacy and structured content formats\\n   119\\t- Manages tool call/result pairing for proper UI display\\n   120\\t- Provides automatic session ID generation with timestamp and random suffix pattern\\n   121\\t\\n   122\\t### 3.2. Main Component (`CoderPane`)\\n   123\\t\\n   124\\tLocated at `src/components/coder/CoderPane.tsx`, this is the main orchestrator component:\\n   125\\t\\n   126\\t```typescript\\n   127\\texport interface CoderPaneProps {\\n   128\\t  paneId: string;\\n   129\\t  sessionId?: string; // Passed from pane content\\n   130\\t  titleBarButtonsRef?: { current: any; set: (value: any) => void }; // For dynamic title bar\\n   131\\t}\\n   132\\t```\\n   133\\t\\n   134\\t**Key Responsibilities:**\\n   135\\t\\n   136\\t- **Hook Integration:** Uses `useCoderChat` for all chat functionality\\n   137\\t- **UI Layout:** Structures the pane with message list and input areas\\n   138\\t- **Menu Management:** Creates and manages the History dropdown menu in the title bar\\n   139\\t- **Telemetry:** Tracks user interactions (opening, closing, new chats)\\n   140\\t- **Keyboard Handling:** Implements Escape key to close functionality\\n   141\\t- **Multi-Pane Support:** Handles Cmd/Ctrl+Click for opening items in new panes\\n   142\\t\\n   143\\t**Title Bar Features:**\\n   144\\t\\n   145\\t- **New Chat Button:** Creates fresh session, supports Cmd/Ctrl+Click for new pane\\n   146\\t- **History Menu:** Shows 5 most recent sessions with timestamps and preview\\n   147\\t  - Format: \\\"MM/DD/YY HH:MM | session-id...\\\"\\n   148\\t  - Normal click: Load in current pane\\n   149\\t  - Cmd/Ctrl+Click: Open in new pane\\n   150\\t\\n   151\\t### 3.3. Input Component (`CoderProseMirrorInput`)\\n   152\\t\\n   153\\tLocated at `src/components/coder/CoderProseMirrorInput.tsx`, handles rich text input:\\n   154\\t\\n   155\\t```typescript\\n   156\\tinterface CoderProseMirrorInputProps {\\n   157\\t  onSubmit: (text: string) => void;\\n   158\\t  disabled?: boolean;\\n   159\\t  focusKey?: number; // To trigger re-focus\\n   160\\t}\\n   161\\t```\\n   162\\t\\n   163\\t**Key Features:**\\n   164\\t\\n   165\\t- **ProseMirror Integration:** Lazy-loaded for performance\\n   166\\t- **Multi-line Support:** Enter submits, Shift+Enter adds line break\\n   167\\t- **Dynamic Height:** Starts at 1 line (44px), expands up to 5 lines (124px), then scrolls\\n   168\\t- **Auto-focus:** Maintains focus after operations like new chat or history load\\n   169\\t- **Text Serialization:** Preserves line breaks and formatting when extracting text\\n   170\\t- **Visual Feedback:** Opacity changes when disabled during streaming\\n   171\\t\\n   172\\t**Implementation Details:**\\n   173\\t\\n   174\\t- Uses `@handlewithcare/react-prosemirror` for React integration\\n   175\\t- Custom keymap for Enter/Shift+Enter handling\\n   176\\t- Document serialization preserves paragraph structure and hard breaks\\n   177\\t- RequestAnimationFrame used for reliable focus management\\n   178\\t- Dynamic sizing: `minHeight: 44px`, `maxHeight: 124px`, `overflowY: auto`\\n   179\\t- Height calculations based on line height (20px) + padding (24px total)\\n   180\\t\\n   181\\t### 3.4. Message Display Components\\n   182\\t\\n   183\\t#### CoderMessage (`src/components/coder/CoderMessage.tsx`)\\n   184\\t\\n   185\\tRenders individual chat messages with appropriate formatting:\\n   186\\t\\n   187\\t```typescript\\n   188\\tinterface CoderMessageProps {\\n   189\\t  message: ChatMessage;\\n   190\\t  index: number;\\n   191\\t}\\n   192\\t```\\n   193\\t\\n   194\\t**Features:**\\n   195\\t\\n   196\\t- **Part-based Rendering:** Handles text, tool calls, and tool results differently\\n   197\\t- **Copy Functionality:** Hover-activated copy button for full message content\\n   198\\t- **Streaming Indicator:** Shows \\\"Claude Code is working\\\" with spinner\\n   199\\t- **Role-based Styling:** Different appearance for user vs assistant messages\\n   200\\t\\n   201\\t#### CoderMessageList (`src/components/coder/CoderMessageList.tsx`)\\n   202\\t\\n   203\\tManages the scrollable message container:\\n   204\\t\\n   205\\t```typescript\\n   206\\tinterface CoderMessageListProps {\\n   207\\t  messages: ChatMessage[];\\n   208\\t  containerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;\\n   209\\t  handleScroll: () => void;\\n   210\\t  handleTouchStart: () => void;\\n   211\\t  isStreamingLastMessage: boolean;\\n   212\\t}\\n   213\\t```\\n   214\\t\\n   215\\t**Features:**\\n   216\\t\\n   217\\t- **Auto-scroll Integration:** Works with `useAutoScroll` hook\\n   218\\t- **System Message Filtering:** Hides internal system messages from view\\n   219\\t- **Responsive Layout:** Centers content with max-width constraint\\n   220\\t\\n   221\\t### 3.5. Tool Display Components\\n   222\\t\\n   223\\t#### ToolCallDisplay (`src/components/coder/ToolCallDisplay.tsx`)\\n   224\\t\\n   225\\tShows Claude's tool invocations:\\n   226\\t\\n   227\\t- Displays tool name and parameters in a collapsible format\\n   228\\t- Loading state with spinner while tool executes\\n   229\\t- Syntax highlighting for JSON parameters\\n   230\\t\\n   231\\t#### ToolResultDisplay (`src/components/coder/ToolResultDisplay.tsx`)\\n   232\\t\\n   233\\tShows results from tool executions:\\n   234\\t\\n   235\\t- Success/error state visualization\\n   236\\t- Collapsible content for large results\\n   237\\t- Special formatting for different content types (file contents, errors, etc.)\\n   238\\t\\n   239\\t## 4. State Management & Data Flow\\n   240\\t\\n   241\\t### 4.1. Session Management\\n   242\\t\\n   243\\tSessions follow a specific lifecycle:\\n   244\\t\\n   245\\t1. **Creation:** Generated with format `ui-coder-${timestamp}-${random}`\\n   246\\t2. **Storage:** Saved in component refs and pane content\\n   247\\t3. **Persistence:** Written to database after each message exchange\\n   248\\t4. **Restoration:** Loaded from database with full message history and tool executions\\n   249\\t\\n   250\\t**Session ID Flow:**\\n   251\\t- `initialSessionId` (prop) → `sessionIdRef` (current) → `lastLoadedSessionIdRef` (tracking)\\n   252\\t- Prevents duplicate loads and ensures consistent state\\n   253\\t\\n   254\\t### 4.2. Message Streaming\\n   255\\t\\n   256\\tThe streaming process for Claude's responses:\\n   257\\t\\n   258\\t1. **Initiation:** User message triggers `sendMessage()`\\n   259\\t2. **Placeholder Creation:** Assistant message with `isStreaming: true`\\n   260\\t3. **Chunk Processing:** \\n   261\\t   ```typescript\\n   262\\t   // Chunks can be plain text or structured data\\n   263\\t   { type: 'text', text: '...' }\\n   264\\t   { type: 'tool_call', id: '...', name: '...', parameters: {...} }\\n   265\\t   { type: 'tool_result', tool_use_id: '...', content: {...} }\\n   266\\t   ```\\n   267\\t4. **Message Updates:** Parts array built incrementally, content accumulated for DB\\n   268\\t5. **Completion:** `isStreaming: false`, final state saved\\n   269\\t\\n   270\\t### 4.3. Tool Call Handling\\n   271\\t\\n   272\\tTool calls follow a specific pattern:\\n   273\\t\\n   274\\t1. **Tool Call Receipt:** Structured data with unique ID\\n   275\\t2. **UI Update:** `ToolCallDisplay` shows immediately with loading state\\n   276\\t3. **Result Association:** Results matched by `tool_use_id`\\n   277\\t4. **Display Update:** `ToolResultDisplay` replaces loading state\\n   278\\t\\n   279\\t**Database Storage:**\\n   280\\t- Tool calls stored in `tool_calls_json` column\\n   281\\t- Results stored in separate `tool_executions` table\\n   282\\t- Rehydration reconstructs the full interaction\\n   283\\t\\n   284\\t## 5. User Interactions\\n   285\\t\\n   286\\t### 5.1. Creating New Chat Sessions\\n   287\\t\\n   288\\t**Standard New Chat:**\\n   289\\t1. Click \\\"New Chat\\\" button\\n   290\\t2. Current session saved to `closedPanePositions`\\n   291\\t3. New session ID generated\\n   292\\t4. Messages cleared, editor focused\\n   293\\t\\n   294\\t**New Chat in New Pane (Cmd/Ctrl+Click):**\\n   295\\t1. Hold Cmd/Ctrl while clicking\\n   296\\t2. New pane created at offset position\\n   297\\t3. Fresh session started in new pane\\n   298\\t4. Original pane unchanged\\n   299\\t\\n   300\\t### 5.2. Loading Chat History\\n   301\\t\\n   302\\t**From History Menu:**\\n   303\\t1. Click \\\"History\\\" dropdown\\n   304\\t2. Menu fetches 5 most recent sessions\\n   305\\t3. Select session to load\\n   306\\t4. Messages and tool executions restored\\n   307\\t5. Scroll to bottom, focus editor\\n   308\\t\\n   309\\t**Via Session ID (Programmatic):**\\n   310\\t1. Pane created with specific `sessionId` in content\\n   311\\t2. `useEffect` triggers load on mount\\n   312\\t3. Full conversation restored\\n   313\\t\\n   314\\t### 5.3. Sending Messages\\n   315\\t\\n   316\\t1. Type in ProseMirror editor (multi-line supported)\\n   317\\t2. Press Enter to send (Shift+Enter for new line)\\n   318\\t3. Message added to UI immediately\\n   319\\t4. Streaming response begins\\n   320\\t5. Tool calls/results displayed as received\\n   321\\t6. Final message saved to database\\n   322\\t\\n   323\\t### 5.4. Keyboard Shortcuts\\n   324\\t\\n   325\\t- **Escape:** Close the coder pane (when focused)\\n   326\\t- **Enter:** Send message\\n   327\\t- **Shift+Enter:** New line in message\\n   328\\t- **Cmd/Ctrl+Click:** Open items in new pane (buttons and menu items)\\n   329\\t\\n   330\\t## 6. Database Integration\\n   331\\t\\n   332\\tThe Coder Pane integrates deeply with the DatabaseService:\\n   333\\t\\n   334\\t**Tables Used:**\\n   335\\t- `sessions`: Stores session metadata\\n   336\\t- `messages`: Stores all messages with role, content, and timestamps\\n   337\\t- `tool_executions`: Stores tool call details and results\\n   338\\t\\n   339\\t**Key Operations:**\\n   340\\t\\n   341\\t```typescript\\n   342\\t// Loading messages for a session\\n   343\\tdb.getMessagesForSession(sessionId, limit)\\n   344\\t\\n   345\\t// Getting tool executions for messages\\n   346\\tdb.getToolCallsForMessage(messageId)\\n   347\\t\\n   348\\t// Saving is handled by Claude Code bridge service\\n   349\\t// which writes to DB after processing\\n   350\\t```\\n   351\\t\\n   352\\t**Message Content Formats:**\\n   353\\t\\n   354\\t1. **Plain Text:** Simple string content\\n   355\\t2. **Structured Parts:** JSON with `{ parts: [...] }` for complex messages\\n   356\\t3. **Legacy Tool Calls:** Separate `tool_calls_json` column\\n   357\\t4. **Modern Format:** Array of typed parts in content field\\n   358\\t\\n   359\\t## 7. Styling and Appearance\\n   360\\t\\n   361\\tThe Coder Pane has distinct visual styling:\\n   362\\t\\n   363\\t**Container:**\\n   364\\t- Black background with high opacity\\n   365\\t- Full height flex layout\\n   366\\t- Custom scrollbar styling\\n   367\\t\\n   368\\t**Messages:**\\n   369\\t- User messages: 80% max width, white border\\n   370\\t- Assistant messages: 100% width, no border\\n   371\\t- Monospace font throughout\\n   372\\t- White text on black background\\n   373\\t\\n   374\\t**Tool Displays:**\\n   375\\t- Muted borders and backgrounds\\n   376\\t- Collapsible with smooth transitions\\n   377\\t- Syntax highlighting for code/JSON\\n   378\\t\\n   379\\t**Editor:**\\n   380\\t- Dynamic height: starts at 44px (1 line), expands up to 124px (5 lines)\\n   381\\t- Internal scrolling when content exceeds 5 lines\\n   382\\t- White border\\n   383\\t- 750px max width (matching message area)\\n   384\\t- Opacity feedback when disabled\\n   385\\t\\n   386\\t**CSS-in-JS Styles:**\\n   387\\tThe component includes embedded styles for prose formatting, ensuring consistent appearance across all message types.\\n   388\\t\\n   389\\t## 8. Testing Considerations\\n   390\\t\\n   391\\t**Unit Tests:**\\n   392\\t- `useCoderChat` hook logic (state updates, API calls)\\n   393\\t- Message parsing and formatting functions\\n   394\\t- Session ID generation and validation\\n   395\\t\\n   396\\t**Integration Tests:**\\n   397\\t- Database operations (save/load cycles)\\n   398\\t- Streaming message handling\\n   399\\t- Tool call/result association\\n   400\\t\\n   401\\t**E2E Tests:**\\n   402\\t- Full conversation flow\\n   403\\t- History menu interactions\\n   404\\t- Multi-pane scenarios\\n   405\\t- Keyboard shortcuts\\n   406\\t\\n   407\\t**Key Test Scenarios:**\\n   408\\t1. Create new chat → Send message → Receive response → Close → Reopen → Verify persistence\\n   409\\t2. Open history → Load session → Verify content → Send follow-up\\n   410\\t3. Cmd+Click new chat → Verify independent panes\\n   411\\t4. Stream interruption → Verify graceful handling\\n   412\\t\\n   413\\t## 9. Future Enhancements\\n   414\\t\\n   415\\t**Potential Improvements:**\\n   416\\t\\n   417\\t1. **Search Functionality:** Search across all chat history\\n   418\\t2. **Export Options:** Save conversations as markdown/PDF\\n   419\\t3. **Model Selection:** Choose between different Claude models\\n   420\\t4. **Code Block Actions:** Copy, run, or save code snippets directly\\n   421\\t5. **Collaborative Sessions:** Share sessions with team members\\n   422\\t6. **Voice Input:** Dictate messages instead of typing\\n   423\\t7. **Theme Customization:** Light mode support, font size options\\n   424\\t8. **Performance Optimization:** Virtual scrolling for very long conversations\\n   425\\t9. **Advanced Tool Displays:** Interactive file trees, inline editors\\n   426\\t10. **Session Templates:** Pre-configured prompts for common tasks\\n   427\\t\\n   428\\t**Technical Debt:**\\n   429\\t- Consolidate message format handling (legacy vs modern)\\n   430\\t- Optimize database queries for large histories\\n   431\\t- Implement message pagination for performance\\n   432\\t- Add retry logic for failed API calls\\n   433\\t\\n   434\\tThe Coder Pane represents a sophisticated integration of AI assistance, real-time streaming, persistent storage, and intuitive UI design, providing developers with a powerful tool for AI-assisted coding within the OpenAgents Commander environment.\"}",

[Testing Strategies](#10-testing-strategies)\n    24\t11. [Resilient Session Management (NEW)](#11-resilient-session-management-new)\n    25\t    11.1. [Overview](#111-overview)\n    26\t    11.2. [Future Considerations](#13-future-considerations)\n    31\t\n    32\t## 1. Overview\n    33\t\n    34\tThe Claude Code CLI Integration & Bridge Service system enables OpenAgents Commander to utilize the specialized coding capabilities of the `@anthropic-ai/claude-code` command-line tool. Due to Electron's security model and the complexities of managing long-running, network-dependent subprocesses (as highlighted in `docs/claude-code/compass_artifact_wf-*.md`), a multi-layered approach involving an external Node.js \"bridge\" service is employed.\n    35\t\n    36\tThis system allows Commander to:\n    37\t-   Invoke the Claude Code CLI for chat completions, including streaming responses.\n    38\t-   Integrate Claude Code CLI as a provider within the application's unified `AgentLanguageModel` AI backend.\n    39\t-   Persist chat sessions and messages related to Claude Code CLI interactions into the shared PGlite database.\n    40\t-   Handle potential tool use (function calling) if supported by the CLI and models.\n    41\t\n    42\tThe architecture prioritizes offloading the direct management of the Claude CLI subprocess to an external Node.js environment, which communicates with the Electron application via WebSockets. This bypasses certain Electron limitations and provides a more robust environment for the CLI tool.\n    43\t\n    44\t## 2. Architecture Diagram\n    45\t\n    46\t(Adapted from `docs/systems/message-persistence-architecture.md`, focusing on Claude Code integration)\n    47\t\n    48\t```\n
